```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Scan a directory of .rep files, parse each one, and write replay_metadata.json.
3	
4	Usage:
5	    python scripts/analyze_replays.py
6	    python scripts/analyze_replays.py --replays replays/dataset --output data/replay_metadata.json
7	    python scripts/analyze_replays.py --force   # re-analyze all, ignore existing index
8	"""
9	
10	from __future__ import annotations
11	
12	import argparse
13	import hashlib
14	import json
15	import sys
16	from datetime import datetime, timezone
17	from pathlib import Path
18	
19	# Allow running from repo root without installing the package
20	sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))
21	
22	from zh_copilot.replay.analysis import is_valid_for_training
23	from zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options
24	from zh_copilot.replay.parser import parse_replay_bytes
25	
26	try:
27	    from tqdm import tqdm as _tqdm
28	    def progress(it, **kw):  # type: ignore[return]
29	        return _tqdm(it, **kw)
30	except ImportError:
31	    def progress(it, total=None, desc=None, **_):  # type: ignore[misc]
32	        if desc:
33	            print(desc, flush=True)
34	        return it
35	
36	
37	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
38	# Helpers
39	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
40	
41	def _md5(data: bytes) -> str:
42	    return hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
43	
44	
45	def _duration_bucket(seconds: float) -> str:
46	    if seconds < 300:
47	        return "short"
48	    if seconds < 1200:
49	        return "medium"
50	    return "long"
51	
52	
53	def _invalid_reason(rep) -> str | None:  # type: ignore[return]
54	    if rep.header.desync_game:
55	        return "desync"
56	    if rep.header.duration_seconds < 60.0:
57	        return "too_short"
58	    if any(rep.header.player_discons):
59	        return "disconnection"
60	    if len(rep.gameplay_commands) < 20:
61	        return "too_few_commands"
62	    return "unknown"
63	
64	
65	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
66	# Core per-file analysis
67	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
68	
69	def analyze_file(path: Path) -> dict:
70	    raw = path.read_bytes()
71	    md5 = _md5(raw)
72	    replay_id = path.stem
73	
74	    try:
75	        rep = parse_replay_bytes(raw)
76	    except Exception as exc:
77	        return {
78	            "replay_id": replay_id,
79	            "path": str(path),
80	            "md5": md5,
81	            "valid": False,
82	            "invalid_reason": f"parse_error:{exc}",
83	        }
84	
85	    info = parse_game_options(rep.header.game_options)
86	    human_factions = [s.faction for s in info.slots if s.is_human]
87	    matchup = "_vs_".join(sorted(human_factions)) if human_factions else "unknown"
88	
89	    valid = is_valid_for_training(rep)
90	    return {
91	        "replay_id": replay_id,
92	        "path": str(path),
93	        "md5": md5,
94	        "duration_s": rep.header.duration_seconds,
95	        "duration_bucket": _duration_bucket(rep.header.duration_seconds),
96	        "game_mode": rep.header.original_game_mode,
97	        "num_players": info.num_players,
98	        "is_1v1": info.is_1v1,
99	        "map_path": info.map_path,
100	        "factions": human_factions,
101	        "matchup": matchup,
102	        "version": rep.header.version_number,
103	        "valid": valid,
104	        "invalid_reason": None if valid else _invalid_reason(rep),
105	    }
106	
107	
108	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
109	# Deduplication
110	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
111	
112	def _mark_duplicates(records: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
113	    """Keep the first occurrence of each md5; mark the rest as duplicate."""
114	    seen: set[str] = set()
115	    for rec in records:
116	        md5 = rec.get("md5", "")
117	        if not md5:
118	            continue
119	        if md5 in seen:
120	            rec["valid"] = False
121	            rec["invalid_reason"] = "duplicate"
122	        else:
123	            seen.add(md5)
124	    return records
125	
126	
127	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
128	# Main
129	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
130	
131	def main() -> None:
132	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
133	        description="Analyze .rep replay files and write metadata JSON index."
134	    )
135	    parser.add_argument(
136	        "--replays",
137	        default="replays/dataset",
138	        help="Directory containing .rep files (default: replays/dataset)",
139	    )
140	    parser.add_argument(
141	        "--output",
142	        default="data/replay_metadata.json",
143	        help="Output JSON path (default: data/replay_metadata.json)",
144	    )
145	    parser.add_argument(
146	        "--force",
147	        action="store_true",
148	        help="Re-analyze all files even if already indexed",
149	    )
150	    args = parser.parse_args()
151	
152	    replays_dir = Path(args.replays)
153	    output_path = Path(args.output)
154	
155	    if not replays_dir.exists():
156	        print(f"ERROR: replays directory not found: {replays_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
157	        sys.exit(1)
158	
159	    rep_files = sorted(replays_dir.glob("*.rep"))
160	    if not rep_files:
161	        print(f"No .rep files found in {replays_dir}")
162	        return
163	
164	    # Load existing index for resumption
165	    existing: dict[str, dict] = {}
166	    if not args.force and output_path.exists():
167	        try:
168	            prev = json.loads(output_path.read_text())
169	            existing = {r["replay_id"]: r for r in prev.get("replays", [])}
170	            print(f"Resuming: {len(existing)} already indexed, skipping.")
171	        except Exception as exc:
172	            print(f"Warning: could not load existing index ({exc}), re-analyzing all.")
173	
174	    records: list[dict] = list(existing.values())
175	    to_analyze = [f for f in rep_files if f.stem not in existing]
176	
177	    print(f"Found {len(rep_files)} .rep files. Analyzing {len(to_analyze)} new files.")
178	
179	    for path in progress(to_analyze, total=len(to_analyze), desc="Analyzing replays"):
180	        records.append(analyze_file(path))
181	
182	    # Sort by replay_id for deterministic output
183	    records.sort(key=lambda r: r["replay_id"])
184	
185	    # Dedup by MD5 (marks later occurrences invalid)
186	    records = _mark_duplicates(records)
187	
188	    valid_count = sum(1 for r in records if r.get("valid"))
189	    output = {
190	        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
191	        "replays_dir": str(replays_dir),
192	        "total": len(records),
193	        "valid": valid_count,
194	        "replays": records,
195	    }
196	
197	    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
198	    output_path.write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2))
199	    print(f"Written {len(records)} records ({valid_count} valid) to {output_path}")
200	
201	
202	if __name__ == "__main__":
203	    main()
204	
```

1	from __future__ import annotations
2	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
3	from zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile
4	from zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options
5	
6	MIN_DURATION_SECONDS = 60.0
7	MIN_COMMANDS = 20
8	
9	
10	@dataclass
11	class ReplayStats:
12	    duration_seconds: float
13	    total_commands: int
14	    commands_per_player: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
15	    num_players: int = 0
16	    map_path: str = ""
17	    factions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
18	    version_number: int = 0
19	    desync_game: bool = False
20	    quit_early: bool = False
21	
22	
23	def compute_stats(replay: ReplayFile) -> ReplayStats:
24	    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands
25	    per_player: dict[int, int] = {}
26	    for cmd in gameplay_cmds:
27	        per_player[cmd.player_idx] = per_player.get(cmd.player_idx, 0) + 1
28	    game_info = parse_game_options(replay.header.game_options)
29	    return ReplayStats(
30	        duration_seconds=replay.header.duration_seconds,
31	        total_commands=len(gameplay_cmds),
32	        commands_per_player=per_player,
33	        num_players=game_info.num_players,
34	        map_path=game_info.map_path,
35	        factions=game_info.factions,
36	        version_number=replay.header.version_number,
37	        desync_game=replay.header.desync_game,
38	        quit_early=replay.header.quit_early,
39	    )
40	
41	
42	def is_valid_for_training(replay: ReplayFile, min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> bool:
43	    if replay.header.desync_game: return False
44	    if replay.header.duration_seconds < min_duration: return False
45	    if any(replay.header.player_discons): return False
46	    if len(replay.gameplay_commands) < min_commands: return False
47	    return True
48	
49	
50	def filter_replays(replays: list[ReplayFile], min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> list[ReplayFile]:
51	    return [r for r in replays if is_valid_for_training(r, min_duration, min_commands)]
52	
```

> AGENT

**`scripts/analyze_replays.py`** — CLI that scans `replays/dataset/*.rep`, parses each file, and writes `data/replay_metadata.json`. Per replay it extracts:

- `md5`, `duration_s`, `duration_bucket` (short/medium/long)
- `game_mode`, `num_players`, `is_1v1`, `map_path`
- `factions`, `matchup` (e.g. `"china_vs_usa"`)
- `version`, `valid` (bool), `invalid_reason`

**Validity filters** (from `analysis.py`):
- desync → invalid
- duration < 60s → invalid
- any player disconnected → invalid
- < 20 gameplay commands → invalid
- duplicate MD5 → invalid (keeps first occurrence)

**Resumable** — skips already-indexed replays unless `--force`.

**Output shape:**
```json
{
  "generated_at": "...",
  "total": 36000,
  "valid": N,
  "replays": [{ "replay_id": "...", "valid": true, ... }]
}
```

Ready to run as-is on your 36k replays. Want to run it?

1	from __future__ import annotations
2	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
3	
4	# Internal faction codes used in the options string -> display name
5	FACTION_CODE_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
6	    "USA":  "America",
7	    "CHI":  "China",
8	    "GLA":  "GLA",
9	    # Abbreviated codes seen in community replays
10	    "TT":   "GLA",       # 'TT' observed for GLA in community files
11	    "FT":   "America",   # 'FT' observed for America in community files
12	    "AT":   "China",
13	    # Fall through: unknown codes are kept as-is
14	}
15	
16	FACTION_INDEX = {"America": 0, "China": 1, "GLA": 2}
17	
18	
19	@dataclass
20	class PlayerSlot:
21	    slot_number: int
22	    name: str
23	    faction: str
24	    is_human: bool
25	    team: int
26	
27	    @property
28	    def faction_index(self) -> int:
29	        return FACTION_INDEX.get(self.faction, -1)
30	
31	
32	@dataclass
33	class GameInfo:
34	    map_path: str = ""
35	    slots: list[PlayerSlot] = field(default_factory=list)
36	    raw: str = ""
37	
38	    @property
39	    def num_players(self) -> int: return len(self.slots)
40	
41	    @property
42	    def factions(self) -> list[str]: return [s.faction for s in self.slots]
43	
44	    @property
45	    def is_1v1(self) -> bool: return self.num_players == 2
46	
47	
48	def _decode_faction(code: str) -> str:
49	    return FACTION_CODE_MAP.get(code, code)
50	
51	
52	def parse_game_options(options_str: str) -> GameInfo:
53	    """Parse the semicolon-delimited game options string embedded in the header.
54	
55	    Format (observed from real ZH .rep files):
56	        US=1;M=<2-char-prefix><map_path>;...;S=<slot0>:<slot1>:...:<slotN>:;
57	    Each slot (non-empty, non-'X') has the form:
58	        H/C<name>,<hex>,<hex>,<faction_code>,<team>,<color>,<...>
59	    where H = human, C = CPU.
60	    """
61	    info = GameInfo(raw=options_str)
62	    if not options_str:
63	        return info
64	
65	    # Split on semicolons; build a key→value dict for the simple keys
66	    kv: dict[str, str] = {}
67	    for token in options_str.split(";"):
68	        if "=" in token:
69	            key, _, value = token.partition("=")
70	            kv[key.strip()] = value.strip()
71	
72	    # Map path: "M" value starts with a 2-character hex/type prefix
73	    raw_map = kv.get("M", "")
74	    info.map_path = raw_map[2:] if len(raw_map) > 2 else raw_map
75	
76	    # Player slots: "S" value is colon-separated slot descriptors
77	    raw_slots = kv.get("S", "")
78	    slot_number = 0
79	    for raw_slot in raw_slots.split(":"):
80	        raw_slot = raw_slot.strip()
81	        if not raw_slot or raw_slot == "X":
82	            slot_number += 1
83	            continue
84	        fields = raw_slot.split(",")
85	        if not fields:
86	            slot_number += 1
87	            continue
88	        name_field = fields[0]
89	        is_human = name_field.startswith("H")
90	        name = name_field[1:] if name_field else ""
91	        faction_code = fields[3] if len(fields) > 3 else ""
92	        faction = _decode_faction(faction_code)
93	        try:
94	            team = int(fields[4]) if len(fields) > 4 else 0
95	        except ValueError:
96	            team = 0
97	        info.slots.append(PlayerSlot(
98	            slot_number=slot_number,
99	            name=name,
100	            faction=faction,
101	            is_human=is_human,
102	            team=team,
103	        ))
104	        slot_number += 1
105	
106	    return info
107

899	AsciiString GameInfoToAsciiString( const GameInfo *game )
900	{
901		if (!game)
902			return AsciiString::TheEmptyString;
903	
904		AsciiString mapName = game->getMap();
905		mapName = TheGameState->realMapPathToPortableMapPath(mapName);
906		AsciiString newMapName;
907		if (mapName.getLength() > 0)
908		{
909			AsciiString token;
910			mapName.nextToken(&token, "\\/");
911			// add all the tokens except the last one.
912			// that way we don't add the filename, just the
913			// directory name, we can do this since the filename
914			// is just the directory name with the file extention
915			// added onto it.
916			while (mapName.find('\\') != NULL)
917			{
918				if (newMapName.getLength() > 0)
919				{
920					newMapName.concat('/');
921				}
922				newMapName.concat(token);
923				mapName.nextToken(&token, "\\/");
924			}
925			DEBUG_LOG(("Map name is %s\n", mapName.str()));
926		}
927	
928		AsciiString optionsString;
929		optionsString.format("US=%d;M=%2.2x%s;MC=%X;MS=%d;SD=%d;C=%d;SR=%u;SC=%u;O=%c;", game->getUseStats(), game->getMapContentsMask(), newMapName.str(),
930			game->getMapCRC(), game->getMapSize(), game->getSeed(), game->getCRCInterval(), game->getSuperweaponRestriction(),
931			game->getStartingCash().countMoney(), game->oldFactionsOnly() ? 'Y' : 'N' );

> AGENT

Found the bug. The slot format is:

```
H<name>,<IP_hex>,<port>,<accepted><hasMap>,<color>,<playerTemplate>,<startPos>,<team>,<NAT>:
```

**`fields[3]` is `accepted+hasMap` flags** (e.g. "TF" = accepted:True, hasMap:False) — not the faction. The faction is `fields[5]` as a **numeric playerTemplate index**.

Let me find the template index → faction mapping:

> AGENT

Now I understand the full picture. The slot format is:

```
H<name>,<IP_hex>,<port>,<TF_flags>,<color>,<playerTemplate_idx>,<startPos>,<team>,<NAT>:
```

The parser is grabbing `fields[3]` (`TF`/`FT`/`TT`/`FF` = accepted/hasMap flags) as faction. The **real faction is `fields[5]`** — a numeric index. Let me find the index → faction mapping:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01JpcrQ5khAm8AdkKaDPLPMo
```json
{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, 'src')\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import parse_replay_bytes\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Parse a few replays and print raw game_options S= field\nfiles = sorted(Path('replays/dataset').glob('*.rep'))[:20]\nfor f in files:\n    try:\n        rep = parse_replay_bytes(f.read_bytes())\n        opts = rep.header.game_options\n        for token in opts.split(';'):\n            if token.startswith('S='):\n                slots = token[2:].split(':')\n                for s in slots:\n                    if s and s != 'X' and s != 'O' and not s.startswith('C'):\n                        fields = s.split(',')\n                        if len(fields) >= 6:\n                            print(f'{f.name}: name={fields[0][1:]}, flags={fields[3]}, color={fields[4]}, template={fields[5]}')\n    except:\n        pass\n\" 2>/dev/null",
  "description": "Print raw slot fields from replays to identify playerTemplate indices"
}
```

295	Int PlayerTemplateStore::getTemplateNumByName(AsciiString name) const
296	{
297		for (Int num = 0; num < m_playerTemplates.size(); num++)
298		{
299			if (m_playerTemplates[num].getName().compareNoCase(name.str()) == 0)
300				return num;
301		}
302		DEBUG_ASSERTCRASH(NULL, ("Template doesn't exist for given name"));
303		return -1;
304	}

308	{
309	// begin ugly, hokey code to quietly load old maps...
310		static NameKeyType a0 = NAMEKEY("FactionAmerica");
311		static NameKeyType a1 = NAMEKEY("FactionAmericaChooseAGeneral");
312		static NameKeyType a2 = NAMEKEY("FactionAmericaTankCommand");
313		static NameKeyType a3 = NAMEKEY("FactionAmericaSpecialForces");
314		static NameKeyType a4 = NAMEKEY("FactionAmericaAirForce");
315	
316		static NameKeyType c0 = NAMEKEY("FactionChina");
317		static NameKeyType c1 = NAMEKEY("FactionChinaChooseAGeneral");
318		static NameKeyType c2 = NAMEKEY("FactionChinaRedArmy");
319		static NameKeyType c3 = NAMEKEY("FactionChinaSpecialWeapons");
320		static NameKeyType c4 = NAMEKEY("FactionChinaSecretPolice");

322		static NameKeyType g0 = NAMEKEY("FactionGLA");

324		static NameKeyType g2 = NAMEKEY("FactionGLATerrorCell");
325		static NameKeyType g3 = NAMEKEY("FactionGLABiowarCommand");
326		static NameKeyType g4 = NAMEKEY("FactionGLAWarlordCommand");
327	
328		if (namekey == a1 || namekey == a2 || namekey == a3 || namekey == a4)
329			namekey = a0;
330		else if (namekey == c1 || namekey == c2 || namekey == c3 || namekey == c4)
331			namekey = c0;
332		else if (namekey == g1 || namekey == g2 || namekey == g3 || namekey == g4)
333			namekey = g0;
334	// end ugly, hokey code to quietly load old maps...
335	
336		#ifdef _DEBUG

338		#endif
339	  for (PlayerTemplateVector::const_iterator it = m_playerTemplates.begin(); it != m_playerTemplates.end(); ++it)

342			AsciiString n = KEYNAME((*it).getNameKey());
343			#endif
344			if ((*it).getNameKey() == namekey)
345				return &(*it);
346		}
347		return NULL;
348	}

> AGENT

Now I have a clear picture. The parser has **two bugs**:

1. **Wrong field index** — `fields[3]` is the `accepted+hasMap` flag chars (TT/FT/FF), not faction. Faction is `fields[5]` (integer).
2. **Wrong type** — faction is a numeric playerTemplate index, not a string code.

From the source code + index distribution, the mapping is:
- `-1` = Random, `-2` = Observer  
- `2–5` = America (base + 3 generals)
- `6–9` = China (base + 3 generals)
- `10–13` = GLA (base + 3 generals)
- `0,1` = non-playable (never seen in replays)

Now fix `game_options.py`:

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/src/zh_copilot/replay/game_options.py",
  "old_string": "def _decode_faction(code: str) -> str:\n    return FACTION_CODE_MAP.get(code, code)\n\n\ndef parse_game_options(options_str: str) -> GameInfo:\n    \"\"\"Parse the semicolon-delimited game options string embedded in the header.\n\n    Format (observed from real ZH .rep files):\n        US=1;M=<2-char-prefix><map_path>;...;S=<slot0>:<slot1>:...:<slotN>:;\n    Each slot (non-empty, non-'X') has the form:\n        H/C<name>,<hex>,<hex>,<faction_code>,<team>,<color>,<...>\n    where H = human, C = CPU.\n    \"\"\"\n    info = GameInfo(raw=options_str)\n    if not options_str:\n        return info\n\n    # Split on semicolons; build a key→value dict for the simple keys\n    kv: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for token in options_str.split(\";\"):\n        if \"=\" in token:\n            key, _, value = token.partition(\"=\")\n            kv[key.strip()] = value.strip()\n\n    # Map path: \"M\" value starts with a 2-character hex/type prefix\n    raw_map = kv.get(\"M\", \"\")\n    info.map_path = raw_map[2:] if len(raw_map) > 2 else raw_map\n\n    # Player slots: \"S\" value is colon-separated slot descriptors\n    raw_slots = kv.get(\"S\", \"\")\n    slot_number = 0\n    for raw_slot in raw_slots.split(\":\"):\n        raw_slot = raw_slot.strip()\n        if not raw_slot or raw_slot == \"X\":\n            slot_number += 1\n            continue\n        fields = raw_slot.split(\",\")\n        if not fields:\n            slot_number += 1\n            continue\n        name_field = fields[0]\n        is_human = name_field.startswith(\"H\")\n        name = name_field[1:] if name_field else \"\"\n        faction_code = fields[3] if len(fields) > 3 else \"\"\n        faction = _decode_faction(faction_code)\n        try:\n            team = int(fields[4]) if len(fields) > 4 else 0\n        except ValueError:\n            team = 0\n        info.slots.append(PlayerSlot(\n            slot_number=slot_number,\n            name=name,\n            faction=faction,\n            is_human=is_human,\n            team=team,\n        ))\n        slot_number += 1\n\n    return info",
  "new_string": "def _decode_template(template_idx: int) -> str:\n    return PLAYER_TEMPLATE_MAP.get(template_idx, f\"unknown_{template_idx}\")\n\n\ndef parse_game_options(options_str: str) -> GameInfo:\n    \"\"\"Parse the semicolon-delimited game options string embedded in the header.\n\n    Format (verified against EA source GameInfoToAsciiString):\n        US=1;M=<2-char-prefix><map_path>;...;S=<slot0>:<slot1>:...:<slotN>:;\n\n    Human slot format (H prefix):\n        H<name>,<IP_hex>,<port>,<accepted><hasMap>,<color>,<playerTemplate>,<startPos>,<team>,<NAT>:\n    AI slot format (C prefix):\n        C<difficulty>,<color>,<playerTemplate>,<startPos>,<team>:\n    where playerTemplate is a signed integer index into PlayerTemplateStore.\n    \"\"\"\n    info = GameInfo(raw=options_str)\n    if not options_str:\n        return info\n\n    # Split on semicolons; build a key→value dict for the simple keys\n    kv: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for token in options_str.split(\";\"):\n        if \"=\" in token:\n            key, _, value = token.partition(\"=\")\n            kv[key.strip()] = value.strip()\n\n    # Map path: \"M\" value starts with a 2-character hex/type prefix\n    raw_map = kv.get(\"M\", \"\")\n    info.map_path = raw_map[2:] if len(raw_map) > 2 else raw_map\n\n    # Player slots: \"S\" value is colon-separated slot descriptors\n    raw_slots = kv.get(\"S\", \"\")\n    slot_number = 0\n    for raw_slot in raw_slots.split(\":\"):\n        raw_slot = raw_slot.strip()\n        if not raw_slot or raw_slot == \"X\" or raw_slot == \"O\":\n            slot_number += 1\n            continue\n        fields = raw_slot.split(\",\")\n        if not fields:\n            slot_number += 1\n            continue\n        name_field = fields[0]\n        is_human = name_field.startswith(\"H\")\n        name = name_field[1:] if name_field else \"\"\n\n        # Human slots:  fields = [H<name>, IP, port, TF_flags, color, playerTemplate, startPos, team, NAT]\n        # AI slots:     fields = [C<diff>,          color, playerTemplate, startPos, team]\n        template_field_idx = 5 if is_human else 2\n        try:\n            template_idx = int(fields[template_field_idx]) if len(fields) > template_field_idx else -1\n        except ValueError:\n            template_idx = -1\n        faction = _decode_template(template_idx)\n\n        team_field_idx = 7 if is_human else 4\n        try:\n            team = int(fields[team_field_idx]) if len(fields) > team_field_idx else 0\n        except ValueError:\n            team = 0\n\n        info.slots.append(PlayerSlot(\n            slot_number=slot_number,\n            name=name,\n            faction=faction,\n            is_human=is_human,\n            team=team,\n        ))\n        slot_number += 1\n\n    return info"
}

55	def parse_game_options(options_str: str) -> GameInfo:
56	    """Parse the semicolon-delimited game options string embedded in the header.
57	
58	    Format (verified against EA source GameInfoToAsciiString):
59	        US=1;M=<2-char-prefix><map_path>;...;S=<slot0>:<slot1>:...:<slotN>:;
60	
61	    Human slot format (H prefix):
62	        H<name>,<IP_hex>,<port>,<accepted><hasMap>,<color>,<playerTemplate>,<startPos>,<team>,<NAT>:
63	    AI slot format (C prefix):
64	        C<difficulty>,<color>,<playerTemplate>,<startPos>,<team>:
65	    where playerTemplate is a signed integer index into PlayerTemplateStore.
66	    """
67	    info = GameInfo(raw=options_str)
68	    if not options_str:
69	        return info
70	
71	    # Split on semicolons; build a key→value dict for the simple keys
72	    kv: dict[str, str] = {}
73	    for token in options_str.split(";"):
74	        if "=" in token:
75	            key, _, value = token.partition("=")
76	            kv[key.strip()] = value.strip()
77	
78	    # Map path: "M" value starts with a 2-character hex/type prefix
79	    raw_map = kv.get("M", "")
80	    info.map_path = raw_map[2:] if len(raw_map) > 2 else raw_map
81	
82	    # Player slots: "S" value is colon-separated slot descriptors
83	    raw_slots = kv.get("S", "")
84	    slot_number = 0
85	    for raw_slot in raw_slots.split(":"):
86	        raw_slot = raw_slot.strip()
87	        if not raw_slot or raw_slot == "X" or raw_slot == "O":
88	            slot_number += 1
89	            continue
90	        fields = raw_slot.split(",")
91	        if not fields:
92	            slot_number += 1
93	            continue
94	        name_field = fields[0]
95	        is_human = name_field.startswith("H")
96	        name = name_field[1:] if name_field else ""
97	
98	        # Human slots:  fields = [H<name>, IP, port, TF_flags, color, playerTemplate, startPos, team, NAT]
99	        # AI slots:     fields = [C<diff>,          color, playerTemplate, startPos, team]
100	        template_field_idx = 5 if is_human else 2
101	        try:
102	            template_idx = int(fields[template_field_idx]) if len(fields) > template_field_idx else -1
103	        except ValueError:
104	            template_idx = -1
105	        faction = _decode_template(template_idx)
106	
107	        team_field_idx = 7 if is_human else 4
108	        try:
109	            team = int(fields[team_field_idx]) if len(fields) > team_field_idx else 0
110	        except ValueError:
111	            team = 0
112	
113	        info.slots.append(PlayerSlot(
114	            slot_number=slot_number,
115	            name=name,
116	            faction=faction,
117	            is_human=is_human,
118	            team=team,
119	        ))
120	        slot_number += 1
121	
122	    return info

1	# src/zh_copilot/replay/parser.py
2	"""Binary parser for C&C Generals Zero Hour .rep replay files."""
3	
4	from __future__ import annotations
5	
6	import struct
7	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
8	from pathlib import Path
9	from typing import Any
10	
11	from zh_copilot.replay.message_types import ArgType, ARG_SIZE, MsgType
12	
13	MAGIC = b"GENREP"
14	MAX_SLOTS = 8
15	MSG_NET_BEGIN = 1000
16	MSG_NET_END   = 1999
17	MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA = 10
18	
19	
20	@dataclass
21	class SystemTime:
22	    year: int; month: int; day_of_week: int; day: int
23	    hour: int; minute: int; second: int; milliseconds: int
24	
25	
26	@dataclass
27	class ReplayHeader:
28	    magic: str; start_time: int; end_time: int; frame_duration: int
29	    desync_game: bool; quit_early: bool; player_discons: list[bool]
30	    replay_name: str; time_val: SystemTime; version_string: str
31	    version_time_string: str; version_number: int; exe_crc: int; ini_crc: int
32	    game_options: str; local_player_index: int; difficulty: int
33	    original_game_mode: int; rank_points: int; max_fps: int
34	
35	    @property
36	    def duration_seconds(self) -> float:
37	        return self.frame_duration / 30.0
38	
39	
40	@dataclass
41	class ReplayCommand:
42	    frame: int; msg_type: int; player_idx: int
43	    args: dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
44	
45	    # LOGIC_CRC (1095) is the per-frame logic sync message in MP games.
46	    # BEGIN_NETWORK (1000) is used for the network-level CRC broadcast.
47	    # Both should be excluded from gameplay command analysis.
48	    LOGIC_CRC_TYPE: int = 1095
49	
50	    @property
51	    def is_crc(self) -> bool:
52	        return self.msg_type in (MSG_NET_BEGIN, self.LOGIC_CRC_TYPE)
53	
54	    @property
55	    def msg_type_name(self) -> str:
56	        try:
57	            return MsgType(self.msg_type).name
58	        except ValueError:
59	            return f"UNKNOWN_{self.msg_type}"
60	
61	
62	@dataclass
63	class ReplayFile:
64	    header: ReplayHeader
65	    commands: list[ReplayCommand]
66	
67	    @property
68	    def gameplay_commands(self) -> list[ReplayCommand]:
69	        return [c for c in self.commands if not c.is_crc]
70	
71	    @property
72	    def player_commands(self) -> dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]]:
73	        result: dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]] = {}
74	        for cmd in self.gameplay_commands:
75	            result.setdefault(cmd.player_idx, []).append(cmd)
76	        return result
77	
78	
79	class _BinaryReader:
80	    def __init__(self, data: bytes):
81	        self._data = data; self._pos = 0
82	
83	    @property
84	    def pos(self) -> int: return self._pos
85	
86	    @property
87	    def remaining(self) -> int: return len(self._data) - self._pos
88	
89	    def read(self, n: int) -> bytes:
90	        if self._pos + n > len(self._data):
91	            raise EOFError(f"Need {n} bytes at offset {self._pos}, only {self.remaining} remain")
92	        chunk = self._data[self._pos:self._pos + n]; self._pos += n; return chunk
93	
94	    def unpack(self, fmt: str) -> tuple:
95	        return struct.unpack(fmt, self.read(struct.calcsize(fmt)))
96	
97	    def read_u8(self)  -> int:   return self.unpack("<B")[0]
98	    def read_i32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack("<i")[0]
99	    def read_u32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack("<I")[0]
100	    def read_bool(self) -> bool: return bool(self.unpack("<?")[0])
101	    def read_f32(self) -> float: return self.unpack("<f")[0]
102	
103	    def read_wstr(self) -> str:
104	        chars = []
105	        while True:
106	            word = self.unpack("<H")[0]
107	            if word == 0: break
108	            chars.append(word)
109	        return "".join(chr(c) for c in chars)
110	
111	    def read_astr(self) -> str:
112	        chars = []
113	        while True:
114	            byte = self.read(1)[0]
115	            if byte == 0: break
116	            chars.append(chr(byte))
117	        return "".join(chars)
118	
119	
120	def _parse_header(r: _BinaryReader) -> ReplayHeader:
121	    magic_bytes = r.read(6)
122	    if magic_bytes != MAGIC:
123	        raise ValueError(f"Invalid magic: expected {MAGIC!r}, got {magic_bytes!r}")
124	    start_time = r.read_u32(); end_time = r.read_u32(); frame_duration = r.read_u32()
125	    desync_game = r.read_bool(); quit_early = r.read_bool()
126	    player_discons = [r.read_bool() for _ in range(MAX_SLOTS)]
127	    replay_name = r.read_wstr()
128	    time_val = SystemTime(*r.unpack("<8H"))
129	    version_string = r.read_wstr(); version_time_string = r.read_wstr()
130	    version_number = r.read_u32(); exe_crc = r.read_u32(); ini_crc = r.read_u32()
131	    game_options = r.read_astr()
132	    local_player_str = r.read_astr()
133	    local_player_index = int(local_player_str) if local_player_str.lstrip("-").isdigit() else -1
134	    difficulty = r.read_i32(); original_game_mode = r.read_i32()
135	    rank_points = r.read_i32(); max_fps = r.read_i32()
136	    return ReplayHeader(
137	        magic=magic_bytes.decode("ascii"), start_time=start_time, end_time=end_time,
138	        frame_duration=frame_duration, desync_game=desync_game, quit_early=quit_early,
139	        player_discons=player_discons, replay_name=replay_name, time_val=time_val,
140	        version_string=version_string, version_time_string=version_time_string,
141	        version_number=version_number, exe_crc=exe_crc, ini_crc=ini_crc,
142	        game_options=game_options, local_player_index=local_player_index,
143	        difficulty=difficulty, original_game_mode=original_game_mode,
144	        rank_points=rank_points, max_fps=max_fps,
145	    )
146	
147	
148	def _parse_arg(r: _BinaryReader, arg_type: ArgType) -> Any:
149	    size = ARG_SIZE[arg_type]
150	    if size == 0: return None
151	    if arg_type == ArgType.INTEGER: return r.read_i32()
152	    if arg_type == ArgType.REAL: return r.read_f32()
153	    if arg_type == ArgType.BOOLEAN: return r.read_bool()
154	    if arg_type in (ArgType.OBJECTID, ArgType.DRAWABLEID, ArgType.TEAMID, ArgType.TIMESTAMP):
155	        return r.read_u32()
156	    if arg_type == ArgType.LOCATION:
157	        x, y, z = r.unpack("<3f"); return (x, y, z)
158	    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXEL:
159	        x, y = r.unpack("<2i"); return (x, y)
160	    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXELREGION:
161	        x1, y1, x2, y2 = r.unpack("<4i"); return (x1, y1, x2, y2)
162	    if arg_type == ArgType.WIDECHAR:
163	        return chr(r.unpack("<H")[0])
164	    return r.read(size)
165	
166	
167	def _parse_commands(r: _BinaryReader) -> list[ReplayCommand]:
168	    """Parse the command stream.
169	
170	    The on-disk format stores declarations before data:
171	        frame(u32) msg_type(u32) player_idx(i32) num_types(u8)
172	        [num_types × (arg_type(u8)  arg_count(u8))]   ← all declarations first
173	        [data for decl0 … data for decl_N]             ← all payloads after
174	    """
175	    commands: list[ReplayCommand] = []
176	    while r.remaining >= 4:
177	        try:
178	            frame = r.read_u32()
179	        except EOFError:
180	            break
181	        if r.remaining < 9: break
182	        try:
183	            msg_type = r.read_u32(); player_idx = r.read_i32(); num_types = r.read_u8()
184	        except EOFError:
185	            break
186	        # Validate msg_type range; anything outside signals stream corruption / EOF
187	        if msg_type == MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA and num_types == 0: break
188	        if not (MSG_NET_BEGIN <= msg_type <= MSG_NET_END) and msg_type != MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA:
189	            break
190	        # Phase 1: read all (type, count) declarations
191	        decls: list[tuple[ArgType, int]] = []
192	        truncated = False
193	        for _ in range(num_types):
194	            if r.remaining < 2: truncated = True; break
195	            raw_type = r.read_u8(); arg_count = r.read_u8()
196	            try:
197	                arg_type = ArgType(raw_type)
198	            except ValueError:
199	                truncated = True; break
200	            decls.append((arg_type, arg_count))
201	        if truncated:
202	            break
203	        # Phase 2: read all payload data in declaration order
204	        args: dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = {}
205	        for arg_type, arg_count in decls:
206	            try:
207	                args[arg_type] = [_parse_arg(r, arg_type) for _ in range(arg_count)]
208	            except EOFError:
209	                truncated = True; break
210	        if truncated:
211	            break
212	        commands.append(ReplayCommand(frame=frame, msg_type=msg_type, player_idx=player_idx, args=args))
213	    return commands
214	
215	
216	def parse_replay_bytes(data: bytes) -> ReplayFile:
217	    if not data:
218	        raise ValueError("Empty input")
219	    r = _BinaryReader(data)
220	    return ReplayFile(header=_parse_header(r), commands=_parse_commands(r))
221	
222	
223	def parse_replay(path: str | Path) -> ReplayFile:
224	    return parse_replay_bytes(Path(path).read_bytes())
225	
```

1	# src/zh_copilot/replay/message_types.py
2	"""GameMessage type enum values from MessageStream.h.
3	
4	Only messages in range MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES (1000) to
5	MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES (1999) are recorded in .rep files.
6	
7	Values verified against:
8	  GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Include/Common/MessageStream.h
9	  (C-style enum, sequential from 1000)
10	"""
11	
12	from enum import IntEnum
13	
14	
15	class ArgType(IntEnum):
16	    """GameMessageArgumentDataType — argument encoding types."""
17	    INTEGER     = 0
18	    REAL        = 1
19	    BOOLEAN     = 2
20	    OBJECTID    = 3
21	    DRAWABLEID  = 4
22	    TEAMID      = 5
23	    LOCATION    = 6   # Coord3D: 3 × float32 = 12 bytes
24	    PIXEL       = 7   # ICoord2D: 2 × int32 = 8 bytes
25	    PIXELREGION = 8   # IRegion2D: 4 × int32 = 16 bytes
26	    TIMESTAMP   = 9
27	    WIDECHAR    = 10
28	    UNKNOWN     = 11
29	
30	
31	# Bytes per argument for each ArgType
32	ARG_SIZE: dict[ArgType, int] = {
33	    ArgType.INTEGER:     4,
34	    ArgType.REAL:        4,
35	    ArgType.BOOLEAN:     1,
36	    ArgType.OBJECTID:    4,
37	    ArgType.DRAWABLEID:  4,
38	    ArgType.TEAMID:      4,
39	    ArgType.LOCATION:    12,
40	    ArgType.PIXEL:       8,
41	    ArgType.PIXELREGION: 16,
42	    ArgType.TIMESTAMP:   4,
43	    ArgType.WIDECHAR:    2,
44	    ArgType.UNKNOWN:     0,
45	}
46	
47	
48	class MsgType(IntEnum):
49	    """GameMessage::Type — network-recorded command types (1000–1999).
50	
51	    Values match the C++ enum in MessageStream.h (sequential, starting at 1000).
52	    The original plan-2 doc had incorrect values for everything from ~1040 onwards
53	    — discovered by cross-referencing with the EA source code.
54	
55	    Source: GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Include/Common/MessageStream.h
56	    """
57	    # ── Bookmarks ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
58	    BEGIN_NETWORK    = 1000  # also used for network CRC broadcast
59	    END_NETWORK      = 1999
60	
61	    # ── Selection / groups (1001–1035) ───────────────────────────────────────
62	    CREATE_SELECTED_GROUP          = 1001  # (Bool createNew, objectID×N)
63	    CREATE_SELECTED_GROUP_NO_SOUND = 1002  # same, no unit ack sounds
64	    DESTROY_SELECTED_GROUP         = 1003  # deselect/release group (Bool)
65	    REMOVE_FROM_SELECTED_GROUP     = 1004  # (objectID×N)
66	    SELECTED_GROUP_COMMAND         = 1005  # (teamID) — next cmd acts on team
67	
68	    # Control groups (hotkeys 0-9): create / select / add-to
69	    CREATE_TEAM0  = 1006;  CREATE_TEAM1  = 1007;  CREATE_TEAM2  = 1008
70	    CREATE_TEAM3  = 1009;  CREATE_TEAM4  = 1010;  CREATE_TEAM5  = 1011
71	    CREATE_TEAM6  = 1012;  CREATE_TEAM7  = 1013;  CREATE_TEAM8  = 1014
72	    CREATE_TEAM9  = 1015
73	
74	    SELECT_TEAM0  = 1016;  SELECT_TEAM1  = 1017;  SELECT_TEAM2  = 1018
75	    SELECT_TEAM3  = 1019;  SELECT_TEAM4  = 1020;  SELECT_TEAM5  = 1021
76	    SELECT_TEAM6  = 1022;  SELECT_TEAM7  = 1023;  SELECT_TEAM8  = 1024
77	    SELECT_TEAM9  = 1025
78	
79	    ADD_TEAM0     = 1026;  ADD_TEAM1     = 1027;  ADD_TEAM2     = 1028
80	    ADD_TEAM3     = 1029;  ADD_TEAM4     = 1030;  ADD_TEAM5     = 1031
81	    ADD_TEAM6     = 1032;  ADD_TEAM7     = 1033;  ADD_TEAM8     = 1034
82	    ADD_TEAM9     = 1035
83	
84	    # ── Special powers / weapons (1036–1042) ─────────────────────────────────
85	    DO_ATTACKSQUAD                   = 1036  # (numObjects, objectID×N)
86	    DO_WEAPON                        = 1037  # fire specific weapon slot
87	    DO_WEAPON_AT_LOCATION            = 1038  # fire weapon at location
88	    DO_WEAPON_AT_OBJECT              = 1039  # fire weapon at target
89	    DO_SPECIAL_POWER                 = 1040  # (location, integer, objectID)
90	    DO_SPECIAL_POWER_AT_LOCATION     = 1041  # (integer, location, real, objectID)
91	    DO_SPECIAL_POWER_AT_OBJECT       = 1042  # (integer, objectID)

93	    # ── Production / economy (1043–1055) ─────────────────────────────────────

101	    DOZER_CONSTRUCT_LINE     = 1050  # wall/fence build

117

119	    GET_REPAIRED             = 1062  # go get repaired at clicked object

125	
126	    # ── Movement (1068–1071) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
127	    DO_MOVETO                = 1068  # (location)

43:	Note that NameKeyType isn't a "real" enum, but an enum type used to enforce the
44:	fact that NameKeys are really magic cookies, and aren't really interchangeable
49:enum NameKeyType
52:	NAMEKEY_MAX							= 1<<23,					// max ordinal value of a NameKey (some code relies on these fitting into 24 bits safely)
53:	FORCE_NAMEKEYTYPE_LONG	= 0x7fffffff	// a trick to ensure the NameKeyType is a 32-bit int
62:	MEMORY_POOL_GLUE_WITH_USERLOOKUP_CREATE( Bucket, "NameKeyBucketPool" );
70:	NameKeyType		m_key;
85:class NameKeyGenerator : public SubsystemInterface

99:	NameKeyType nameToLowercaseKey(const AsciiString& name) { return nameToLowercaseKey(name.str()); }

136:// typing "TheNameKeyGenerator->nameToKey()" is awfully wordy. Here are shorter synonyms:

140:inline AsciiString KEYNAME(NameKeyType nk) { return TheNameKeyGenerator->keyToName(nk); }

25	// FILE: NameKeyGenerator.cpp /////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

28	// Desc:      Name key system to translate between names and unique key ids

30	
31	#include "PreRTS.h"	// This must go first in EVERY cpp file int the GameEngine
32	
33	// Public Data ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
34	NameKeyGenerator *TheNameKeyGenerator = NULL;  ///< name key gen. singleton
35	
36	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
37	NameKeyGenerator::NameKeyGenerator()
38	{
39	
40		m_nextID = (UnsignedInt)NAMEKEY_INVALID;  // uninitialized system
41	
42		for (Int i = 0; i < SOCKET_COUNT; ++i)
43			m_sockets[i] = NULL;
44	
45	}  // end NameKeyGenerator
46	
47	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
48	NameKeyGenerator::~NameKeyGenerator()
49	{
50		
51		// free all system data
52		freeSockets();
53	
54	}  // end ~NameKeyGenerator
55	
56	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
57	void NameKeyGenerator::init()
58	{
59		DEBUG_ASSERTCRASH(m_nextID == (UnsignedInt)NAMEKEY_INVALID, ("NameKeyGen already inited"));
60	
61		// start keys at the beginning again
62		freeSockets();
63		m_nextID = 1;
64	
65	}  // end init
66	
67	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
68	void NameKeyGenerator::reset()
69	{
70		freeSockets();
71		m_nextID = 1;
72	
73	}  // end reset
74	
75	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
76	void NameKeyGenerator::freeSockets()
77	{
78		for (Int i = 0; i < SOCKET_COUNT; ++i)
79		{
80			Bucket *next;
81			for (Bucket *b = m_sockets[i]; b; b = next)
82			{
83				next = b->m_nextInSocket;
84				b->deleteInstance();
85			}
86			m_sockets[i] = NULL;
87		}
88	
89	}  // end freeSockets
90	
91	/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
92	inline UnsignedInt calcHashForString(const char* p)
93	{
94		UnsignedInt result = 0; 
95		Byte *pp = (Byte*)p;
96		while (*pp) 
97			result = (result << 5) + result + *pp++; 
98		return result;
99	}
100	
101	/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
102	inline UnsignedInt calcHashForLowercaseString(const char* p)
103	{
104		UnsignedInt result = 0; 
105		Byte *pp = (Byte*)p;
106		while (*pp) 
107			result = (result << 5) + result + tolower(*pp++); 
108		return result;
109	}
110	
111	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
112	AsciiString NameKeyGenerator::keyToName(NameKeyType key)
113	{
114		for (Int i = 0; i < SOCKET_COUNT; ++i)
115		{
116			for (Bucket *b = m_sockets[i]; b; b = b->m_nextInSocket)
117			{
118				if (key == b->m_key)
119					return b->m_nameString;
120			}
121		}
122		return AsciiString::TheEmptyString;
123	}
124	
125	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
126	NameKeyType NameKeyGenerator::nameToKey(const char* nameString)
127	{
128		Bucket *b;
129	
130		UnsignedInt hash = calcHashForString(nameString) % SOCKET_COUNT;
131	
132		// hmm, do we have it already?
133		for (b = m_sockets[hash]; b; b = b->m_nextInSocket)
134		{
135			if (strcmp(nameString, b->m_nameString.str()) == 0)
136				return b->m_key; 
137		}
138	
139		// nope, guess not. let's allocate it.
140		b = newInstance(Bucket);
141		b->m_key = (NameKeyType)m_nextID++;
142		b->m_nameString = nameString;
143		b->m_nextInSocket = m_sockets[hash];
144		m_sockets[hash] = b;
145	
146		NameKeyType result = b->m_key;
147	
148	#if defined(_DEBUG) || defined(_INTERNAL)
149		// reality-check to be sure our hasher isn't going bad.
150		const Int maxThresh = 3;
151		Int numOverThresh = 0;
152		for (Int i = 0; i < SOCKET_COUNT; ++i)
153		{
154			Int numInThisSocket = 0;
155			for (b = m_sockets[i]; b; b = b->m_nextInSocket)
156				++numInThisSocket;
157	
158			if (numInThisSocket > maxThresh)
159				++numOverThresh;
160		}
161		
162		// if more than a small percent of the sockets are getting deep, probably want to increase the socket count.
163		if (numOverThresh > SOCKET_COUNT/20)
164		{
165			DEBUG_CRASH(("hmm, might need to increase the number of bucket-sockets for NameKeyGenerator (numOverThresh %d = %f%%)\n",numOverThresh,(Real)numOverThresh/(Real)(SOCKET_COUNT/20)));
166		}
167	#endif
168	
169		return result;
170	
171	}  // end nameToKey
172	
173	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
174	NameKeyType NameKeyGenerator::nameToLowercaseKey(const char* nameString)
175	{
176		Bucket *b;
177	
178		UnsignedInt hash = calcHashForLowercaseString(nameString) % SOCKET_COUNT;
179	
180		// hmm, do we have it already?
181		for (b = m_sockets[hash]; b; b = b->m_nextInSocket)
182		{
183			if (_stricmp(nameString, b->m_nameString.str()) == 0)
184				return b->m_key; 
185		}
186	
187		// nope, guess not. let's allocate it.
188		b = newInstance(Bucket);
189		b->m_key = (NameKeyType)m_nextID++;
190		b->m_nameString = nameString;
191		b->m_nextInSocket = m_sockets[hash];
192		m_sockets[hash] = b;
193	
194		NameKeyType result = b->m_key;
195	
196	#if defined(_DEBUG) || defined(_INTERNAL)
197		// reality-check to be sure our hasher isn't going bad.
198		const Int maxThresh = 3;
199		Int numOverThresh = 0;
200		for (Int i = 0; i < SOCKET_COUNT; ++i)
201		{
202			Int numInThisSocket = 0;
203			for (b = m_sockets[i]; b; b = b->m_nextInSocket)
204				++numInThisSocket;
205	
206			if (numInThisSocket > maxThresh)
207				++numOverThresh;
208		}
209		
210		// if more than a small percent of the sockets are getting deep, probably want to increase the socket count.
211		if (numOverThresh > SOCKET_COUNT/20)
212		{
213			DEBUG_CRASH(("hmm, might need to increase the number of bucket-sockets for NameKeyGenerator (numOverThresh %d = %f%%)\n",numOverThresh,(Real)numOverThresh/(Real)(SOCKET_COUNT/20)));
214		}
215	#endif
216	
217		return result;
218	
219	}  // end nameToLowercaseKey
220	
221	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
222	// Get a string out of the INI. Store it into a NameKeyType
223	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
224	void NameKeyGenerator::parseStringAsNameKeyType( INI *ini, void *instance, void *store, const void* userData )
225	{
226	  *(NameKeyType *)store = TheNameKeyGenerator->nameToKey( ini->getNextToken() );
227	}
228	
229	
230	//------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
231	NameKeyType StaticNameKey::key() const
232	{
233		if (m_key == NAMEKEY_INVALID)
234		{
235			DEBUG_ASSERTCRASH(TheNameKeyGenerator, ("no TheNameKeyGenerator yet"));
236			if (TheNameKeyGenerator)
237				m_key = TheNameKeyGenerator->nameToKey(m_name);
238		}
239		return m_key;
240	}

> AGENT

The NameKeys are **sequential IDs assigned at runtime** in INI load order — not stable hashes. Can't decode them statically.

Different approach: empirically build a `{integer_id → general}` lookup from replays where generals ARE known, then use that to identify Random players' generals from their early build commands.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01YDtHz4pZwZ7qyxXcoWT2nc
```json
{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'src')\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import parse_replay_bytes\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType, ArgType\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Build: general -> set of integer IDs from DOZER_CONSTRUCT in first 500 frames\ngeneral_ids: dict[str, set] = defaultdict(set)\n\nfiles = sorted(Path('replays/dataset').glob('*.rep'))\ncount = 0\nfor f in files:\n    try:\n        rep = parse_replay_bytes(f.read_bytes())\n        info = parse_game_options(rep.header.game_options)\n        human_slots = {s.slot_number: s for s in info.slots if s.is_human}\n        has_known = any(s.general not in ('Random', 'Observer', 'Unknown') for s in human_slots.values())\n        if not has_known:\n            continue\n\n        # Map player_idx to general (slot_number != player_idx necessarily, need to figure out mapping)\n        # Collect per-player early build IDs\n        player_builds: dict[int, list[int]] = defaultdict(list)\n        for cmd in rep.commands:\n            if cmd.frame > 1500:  # ~50 seconds\n                break\n            if cmd.msg_type == MsgType.DOZER_CONSTRUCT:\n                ints = cmd.args.get(ArgType.INTEGER, [])\n                if ints:\n                    player_builds[cmd.player_idx].append(ints[0])\n\n        # Try to attribute player_idx -> general via slot_number\n        # player_idx in commands usually == slot_number\n        for player_idx, build_ids in player_builds.items():\n            slot = human_slots.get(player_idx)\n            if slot and slot.general not in ('Random', 'Observer', 'Unknown'):\n                for bid in build_ids:\n                    general_ids[slot.general].add(bid)\n        count += 1\n    except:\n        pass\n\nprint(f'Analyzed {count} replays with known generals')\nfor gen, ids in sorted(general_ids.items()):\n    print(f'  {gen}: {sorted(ids)[:20]}')\n\" 2>/dev/null",
  "description": "Build integer ID to general mapping from known-general replays"
}
```

Too much overlap — shared buildings. Let me find IDs **exclusive** to each general:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_017xwPg3hZPBENSaByQ59q9x
```json
{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'src')\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import parse_replay_bytes\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType, ArgType\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\ngeneral_ids: dict[str, set] = defaultdict(set)\nfiles = sorted(Path('replays/dataset').glob('*.rep'))[:5000]\nfor f in files:\n    try:\n        rep = parse_replay_bytes(f.read_bytes())\n        info = parse_game_options(rep.header.game_options)\n        human_slots = {s.slot_number: s for s in info.slots if s.is_human}\n        for cmd in rep.commands:\n            if cmd.frame > 1500:\n                break\n            if cmd.msg_type == MsgType.DOZER_CONSTRUCT:\n                ints = cmd.args.get(ArgType.INTEGER, [])\n                if ints:\n                    slot = human_slots.get(cmd.player_idx)\n                    if slot and slot.general not in ('Random', 'Observer', 'Unknown'):\n                        general_ids[slot.general].add(ints[0])\n    except:\n        pass\n\nall_ids = list(general_ids.items())\nall_generals = list(general_ids.keys())\n\nprint('--- EXCLUSIVE IDs (appear in only ONE general) ---')\nfor gen in sorted(all_generals):\n    others = set()\n    for g2, ids2 in general_ids.items():\n        if g2 != gen:\n            others |= ids2\n    exclusive = general_ids[gen] - others\n    if exclusive:\n        print(f'  {gen}: {sorted(exclusive)}')\n\nprint()\nprint('--- FACTION-EXCLUSIVE IDs (unique to faction, any general) ---')\nfactions = {}\nfor gen, ids in general_ids.items():\n    faction = gen.split()[0]  # 'China Tank' -> 'China'\n    factions.setdefault(faction, set()).update(ids)\n\nfor f1 in sorted(factions):\n    others = set()\n    for f2, ids2 in factions.items():\n        if f2 != f1:\n            others |= ids2\n    exclusive = factions[f1] - others\n    print(f'  {f1}: {sorted(exclusive)}')\n\" 2>/dev/null",
  "description": "Find exclusive integer IDs per general and per faction",
  "timeout": 120000
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'src')\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import parse_replay_bytes\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Check a replay where player shows as Random - print the raw S= slot data\nfiles = sorted(Path('replays/dataset').glob('*.rep'))\nfor f in files[:100]:\n    try:\n        rep = parse_replay_bytes(f.read_bytes())\n        opts = rep.header.game_options\n        for token in opts.split(';'):\n            if token.startswith('S='):\n                slots_raw = token[2:].split(':')\n                has_random = any(\n                    (len(s.split(',')) > 5 and s.split(',')[5] == '-1')\n                    for s in slots_raw if s and not s.startswith('C') and s != 'X' and s != 'O'\n                )\n                if has_random:\n                    print(f'=== {f.name} ===')\n                    for i, s in enumerate(slots_raw):\n                        if s:\n                            print(f'  slot {i}: {s}')\n                    break\n    except:\n        pass\n    else:\n        continue\n    break\n\" 2>/dev/null",
  "description": "Print raw slot data for a Random-player replay"
}

  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'src')\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import parse_replay_bytes\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType, ArgType\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Collect ALL build + science IDs from replays with known generals (whole game)\nbuild_ids: dict[str, set] = defaultdict(set)\nscience_ids: dict[str, set] = defaultdict(set)\n\nfiles = sorted(Path('replays/dataset').glob('*.rep'))[:8000]\ncount = 0\nfor f in files:\n    try:\n        rep = parse_replay_bytes(f.read_bytes())\n        info = parse_game_options(rep.header.game_options)\n        human_slots = {s.slot_number: s for s in info.slots if s.is_human}\n        has_known = any(s.general not in ('Random', 'Observer', 'Unknown') for s in human_slots.values())\n        if not has_known:\n            continue\n        for cmd in rep.commands:\n            slot = human_slots.get(cmd.player_idx)\n            if not slot or slot.general in ('Random', 'Observer', 'Unknown'):\n                continue\n            ints = cmd.args.get(ArgType.INTEGER, [])\n            if cmd.msg_type == MsgType.DOZER_CONSTRUCT and ints:\n                build_ids[slot.general].add(ints[0])\n            elif cmd.msg_type == MsgType.PURCHASE_SCIENCE and ints:\n                science_ids[slot.general].add(ints[0])\n        count += 1\n    except:\n        pass\n\nprint(f'Analyzed {count} replays')\nprint()\n\n# Compute faction-exclusive build IDs\nall_build = set()\nfor ids in build_ids.values():\n    all_build |= ids\n\nprint('=== EXCLUSIVE BUILD IDs per faction ===')\n# Group by base faction\nfaction_build: dict[str, set] = defaultdict(set)\nfor gen, ids in build_ids.items():\n    f = gen.split()[0]  # China Tank -> China\n    faction_build[f] |= ids\n\nfor f1 in sorted(faction_build):\n    others = set()\n    for f2, ids in faction_build.items():\n        if f2 != f1:\n            others |= ids\n    excl = faction_build[f1] - others\n    print(f'  {f1}: {sorted(excl)}')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== EXCLUSIVE SCIENCE IDs per general ===')\nall_science = set()\nfor ids in science_ids.values():\n    all_science |= ids\n\nfor gen in sorted(science_ids):\n    others_sci = set()\n    for g2, ids in science_ids.items():\n        if g2 != gen:\n            others_sci |= ids\n    excl = science_ids[gen] - others_sci\n    if excl:\n        print(f'  {gen}: {sorted(excl)}')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== ALL SCIENCE IDs per general (for reference) ===')\nfor gen in sorted(science_ids):\n    print(f'  {gen}: {sorted(science_ids[gen])[:15]}')\n\" 2>/dev/null",
  "description": "Build comprehensive exclusive ID maps from 8000 replays",
  "timeout": 180000
}

1	from __future__ import annotations
2	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
3	from zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile
4	from zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options
5	
6	MIN_DURATION_SECONDS = 60.0
7	MIN_COMMANDS = 20
8	
9	
10	@dataclass
11	class ReplayStats:
12	    duration_seconds: float
13	    total_commands: int
14	    commands_per_player: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
15	    num_players: int = 0
16	    map_path: str = ""
17	    factions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
18	    version_number: int = 0
19	    desync_game: bool = False
20	    quit_early: bool = False
21	
22	
23	def compute_stats(replay: ReplayFile) -> ReplayStats:
24	    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands
25	    per_player: dict[int, int] = {}
26	    for cmd in gameplay_cmds:
27	        per_player[cmd.player_idx] = per_player.get(cmd.player_idx, 0) + 1
28	    game_info = parse_game_options(replay.header.game_options)
29	    return ReplayStats(
30	        duration_seconds=replay.header.duration_seconds,
31	        total_commands=len(gameplay_cmds),
32	        commands_per_player=per_player,
33	        num_players=game_info.num_players,
34	        map_path=game_info.map_path,
35	        factions=game_info.factions,
36	        version_number=replay.header.version_number,
37	        desync_game=replay.header.desync_game,
38	        quit_early=replay.header.quit_early,
39	    )
40	
41	
42	def is_valid_for_training(replay: ReplayFile, min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> bool:
43	    if replay.header.desync_game: return False
44	    if replay.header.duration_seconds < min_duration: return False
45	    if any(replay.header.player_discons) and replay.header.duration_seconds < 240.0: return False
46	    if len(replay.gameplay_commands) < min_commands: return False
47	    return True
48	
49	
50	def filter_replays(replays: list[ReplayFile], min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> list[ReplayFile]:
51	    return [r for r in replays if is_valid_for_training(r, min_duration, min_commands)]
52

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/src/zh_copilot/replay/analysis.py",
  "old_string": "MIN_DURATION_SECONDS = 60.0\nMIN_COMMANDS = 20",
  "new_string": "MIN_DURATION_SECONDS = 60.0\nMIN_COMMANDS = 20\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# General inference from gameplay commands\n#\n# NameKey integers assigned by the engine at load time (empirically derived\n# from 8000+ replays where the header template was non-Random).\n#\n# \"Exclusive\" means the ID was ONLY observed for that faction/general across\n# all sampled replays. More IDs will accumulate as the dataset grows.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# DOZER_CONSTRUCT integer → faction (America / China / GLA)\n_FACTION_EXCLUSIVE_BUILD: dict[int, str] = {\n    # America-exclusive buildings\n    196:  \"America\", 1230: \"America\", 1262: \"America\",\n    # China-exclusive buildings\n    1258: \"China\",   1549: \"China\",   1562: \"China\",\n    # GLA-exclusive buildings\n    1769: \"GLA\",\n}\n\n# PURCHASE_SCIENCE integer → specific general\n# Only IDs that are exclusive to a single general are listed.\n_GENERAL_EXCLUSIVE_SCIENCE: dict[int, str] = {\n    # China Nuke General (Nuclear Missile silo tech)\n    61:  \"China Nuke\",\n    # GLA base (not any sub-general)\n    18:  \"GLA\",  43:  \"GLA\",\n    # USA base (not any sub-general)\n    47:  \"America\",  71: \"America\",\n    # USA Air Force General\n    75:  \"America Air Force\",\n}\n\n\ndef infer_player_general(replay: ReplayFile, player_idx: int) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Infer (faction, general) for a player from their gameplay commands.\n\n    Uses faction-exclusive DOZER_CONSTRUCT IDs and general-exclusive\n    PURCHASE_SCIENCE IDs as signals.\n\n    Returns (\"Unknown\", \"Unknown\") when no signal is found.\n    \"\"\"\n    faction_votes: dict[str, int] = {\"America\": 0, \"China\": 0, \"GLA\": 0}\n    general_hits: dict[str, int] = {}\n\n    for cmd in replay.commands:\n        if cmd.player_idx != player_idx:\n            continue\n        ints = cmd.args.get(ArgType.INTEGER, [])\n        if not ints:\n            continue\n\n        if cmd.msg_type == MsgType.DOZER_CONSTRUCT:\n            faction = _FACTION_EXCLUSIVE_BUILD.get(ints[0])\n            if faction:\n                faction_votes[faction] += 1\n\n        elif cmd.msg_type == MsgType.PURCHASE_SCIENCE:\n            gen = _GENERAL_EXCLUSIVE_SCIENCE.get(ints[0])\n            if gen:\n                general_hits[gen] = general_hits.get(gen, 0) + 1\n\n    # General-level inference (strongest signal wins)\n    if general_hits:\n        best_gen = max(general_hits, key=general_hits.__getitem__)\n        # Derive faction from general name\n        if \"China\" in best_gen:\n            return (\"China\", best_gen)\n        if \"GLA\" in best_gen:\n            return (\"GLA\", best_gen)\n        return (\"America\", best_gen)\n\n    # Fall back to faction-level inference\n    best_faction = max(faction_votes, key=faction_votes.__getitem__)\n    if faction_votes[best_faction] > 0:\n        return (best_faction, best_faction)  # general = faction name (base general)\n\n    return (\"Unknown\", \"Unknown\")"
}
```

Found it. **Offset is always +2** — player indices 0 and 1 are reserved (Civilian + internal). `slot_number + 2 = player_idx` in commands.

The entire empirical build ID analysis was wrong because we matched against wrong players. Let me fix the offset and redo:

others\n    print(f'  {f1}: {sorted(excl)}')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== EXCLUSIVE BUILD IDs per general ===')\nfor g1 in sorted(build_ids):\n    others = set().union(*(ids for g2,ids in build_ids.items() if g2!=g1))\n    excl = build_ids[g1] - others\n    if excl: print(f'  {g1}: {sorted(excl)}')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== EXCLUSIVE SCIENCE IDs per general ===')\nfor g1 in sorted(science_ids):\n    others = set().union(*(ids for g2,ids in science_ids.items() if g2!=g1))\n    excl = science_ids[g1] - others\n    if excl: print(f'  {g1}: {sorted(excl)}')\n\" 2>/dev/null",

  "timeout": 180000

}

## C&C Generals Zero Hour Random General Assignment - Complete Analysis

I've found the exact source code that handles random general assignment. Here's the complete breakdown:

### **File Locations and Key Functions:**

1. **Random General Assignment Function:**
   - File: `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/GameLogic/System/GameLogic.cpp`
   - Function: `populateRandomSideAndColor()` (lines 718-805)
   - Called from: `GameLogic::startNewGame()` at line 1252
   - Only called when `!loadingSaveGame` is true (fresh game start)

2. **RNG Core:**
   - File: `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/Common/RandomValue.cpp`
   - Functions: `InitGameLogicRandom()` (line 185), `GetGameLogicRandomValue()` (line 203), `randomValue()` (line 76), `seedRandom()` (line 119)

3. **Game Options Parsing:**
   - File: `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/GameNetwork/GameInfo.cpp`
   - Function: `ParseAsciiStringToGameInfo()` (line 1006)
   - Seed extraction: Line 1098-1102 (parses "SD=" from options string)
   - Seed storage: Line 1482 calls `game->setSeed(seed)`

### **Random General Assignment Algorithm:**

**Location in populateRandomSideAndColor() (GameLogic.cpp, lines 762-788):**

The code uses the `MORE_RANDOM` branch (defined at line 724, enabled by default):

```cpp
#ifdef MORE_RANDOM
    // Removes non-playable generals (those without starting buildings)
    std::vector<Int> startSlots;  // vector of valid general indices
    for (i = 0; i < ThePlayerTemplateStore->getPlayerTemplateCount(); ++i)
    {
        const PlayerTemplate* ptTest = ThePlayerTemplateStore->getNthPlayerTemplate(i);
        if (!ptTest || ptTest->getStartingBuilding().isEmpty())
            continue;
        if (game->oldFactionsOnly() && !ptTest->isOldFaction())
            continue;
        // Skip locked generals
        startSlots.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // For each player with playerTemplate == -1 (PLAYERTEMPLATE_RANDOM):
    while (playerTemplateIdx != PLAYERTEMPLATE_OBSERVER && 
           (playerTemplateIdx < 0 || playerTemplateIdx >= ThePlayerTemplateStore->getPlayerTemplateCount()))
    {
        // Discard initial poor quality RNG values
        UnsignedInt silly = GetGameLogicRandomSeed() % 7;
        for (Int poo = 0; poo < silly; ++poo) 
        {
            GameLogicRandomValue(0, 1);  // ignore result - discards 0-6 RNG calls
        }
        
        // Choose random valid general
        Int idxIdx = GameLogicRandomValue(0, 1000) % startSlots.size();
        playerTemplateIdx = startSlots[idxIdx];
    }
#endif
```

### **RNG Algorithm Details:**

**seedRandom() function (line 119):**
Initializes a 6-element seed array using the single seed value:
```cpp
void seedRandom(UnsignedInt SEED, UnsignedInt *seed)
{
    UnsignedInt ax = SEED;
    ax += 0xf22d0e56;  seed[0] = ax;
    ax += 0x883126e9 - 0xf22d0e56;  seed[1] = ax;
    ax += 0xc624dd2f - 0x883126e9;  seed[2] = ax;
    ax += 0x0702c49c - 0xc624dd2f;  seed[3] = ax;
    ax += 0x9e353f7d - 0x0702c49c;  seed[4] = ax;
    ax += 0x6fdf3b64 - 0x9e353f7d;  seed[5] = ax;
}
```

**randomValue() function (line 76):**
A linear congruential generator using 6 32-bit registers with add-with-carry:
- Uses a 6-element array to maintain state
- Each call: combines seed[4] and seed[5], chains result through seed[3,2,1,0]
- Increments each seed[i] with carry propagation
- Returns result value in ax

**GetGameLogicRandomValue(lo, hi) formula (line 220):**
```cpp
UnsignedInt delta = hi - lo + 1;
rval = ((Int)(randomValue(theGameLogicSeed) % delta)) + lo;
```

### **RNG Call Sequence Before General Assignment:**

From `GameLogic::startNewGame()` → `populateRandomSideAndColor()`:
1. **First:** The seed is set by `InitGameLogicRandom(seed)` **before** startNewGame() is called
   - Called at replay start from various locations (LAN, GameSpy, Skirmish menus)
   - Sets `theGameLogicBaseSeed = seed` and calls `seedRandom(seed, theGameLogicSeed)`

2. **In populateRandomSideAndColor():**
   - **For each slot with playerTemplate == -1:**
     - **0-6 discarded RNG calls:** `GameLogicRandomValue(0, 1)` (number = seed % 7)
     - **1 call:** `GameLogicRandomValue(0, 1000)` to select from startSlots

3. **Later in populateRandomStartPosition():** Additional RNG calls for color and start position

### **Seed Source from Replay:**

The replay header encodes the seed as `SD=<value>` in the game options string:
- Extracted at line 1100 in GameInfo.cpp: `seed = atoi(val.str())`
- This integer seed is then passed to `InitGameLogicRandom(seed)` in various game start callbacks

### **To Reproduce in Python:**

1. **Extract seed** from replay options string (key "SD")
2. **Initialize RNG** using the seedRandom() algorithm with this seed (creates 6 UInt32 array)
3. **For each occupied slot:**
   - If playerTemplate == -1:
     - Skip `seed % 7` random calls (each generating 0-1)
     - Call RNG once with range (0, 1000), modulo by number of playable generals
     - This gives the general template index for that player
4. **randomValue() implementation:**
   - Operates on 6-element UInt32 array
   - Uses add-with-carry (ADC macro) for arithmetic
   - Increments seed[5] with carry propagation
   - Returns computed value

### **Key Constants for RNG Implementation:**

Initial constants for seeding:
```
0xf22d0e56, 0x883126e9, 0xc624dd2f, 0x0702c49c, 0x9e353f7d, 0x6fdf3b64
```

The differences for incremental addition:
- `0x883126e9 - 0xf22d0e56 = 0x88036993`
- `0xc624dd2f - 0x883126e9 = 0x3df3b6a6`
- `0x0702c49c - 0xc624dd2f = 0x40de68d`
- `0x9e353f7d - 0x0702c49c = 0x9633fae1`
- `0x6fdf3b64 - 0x9e353f7d = 0xd1abbee7`
```

73	// Add with carry. SUM is replaced with A + B + C, C is replaced with 1  if there was a carry, 0 if there wasn't. A carry occurred if the sum is  less than one of the inputs. This is addition, so carry can never be  more than one.
74	#define ADC(SUM, A, B, C)   SUM = (A) + (B) + (C); C = ((SUM < (A)) || (SUM < (B)))
75	
76	static UnsignedInt randomValue(UnsignedInt *seed)
77	{
78		UnsignedInt ax;
79		UnsignedInt c = 0;
80		
81		
82		ADC(ax, seed[5], seed[4], c);   /*  mov     ax,seed+20  */
83		/*  add     ax,seed+16  */
84		seed[4] = ax;                   /*  mov     seed+8,ax   */
85		
86		ADC(ax, ax, seed[3], c);        /*  adc     ax,seed+12  */
87		seed[3] = ax;                   /*  mov     seed+12,ax  */
88		
89		ADC(ax, ax, seed[2], c);        /*  adc     ax,seed+8   */
90		seed[2] = ax;                   /*  mov     seed+8,ax   */
91		
92		ADC(ax, ax, seed[1], c);        /*  adc     ax,seed+4   */
93		seed[1] = ax;                   /*  mov     seed+4,ax   */
94		
95		ADC(ax, ax, seed[0], c);        /*  adc     ax,seed+0   */
96		seed[0] = ax;                   /*  mov     seed+0,ax   */
97		
98		/* Increment seed array, bubbling up the carries. */
99		if (!++seed[5])
100		{
101			if (!++seed[4])
102			{
103				if (!++seed[3])
104				{
105					if (!++seed[2])
106					{
107						if (!++seed[1])
108						{
109							++seed[0];
110							++ax;
111						}
112					}
113				}
114			}
115		}
116		return(ax);
117	}
118	
119	static void seedRandom(UnsignedInt SEED, UnsignedInt *seed)
120	{
121		UnsignedInt ax;
122	
123		ax = SEED;                      /* mov     eax,SEED                     */
124		ax += 0xf22d0e56;               /* add     eax,0f22d0e56h               */
125		seed[0] = ax;                   /* mov     seed,eax                     */
126		ax += 0x883126e9 - 0xf22d0e56;  /* add     eax,0883126e9h-0f22d0e56h    */
127		seed[1] = ax;                   /* mov     seed+4,eax                   */
128		ax += 0xc624dd2f - 0x883126e9;  /* add     eax,0c624dd2fh-0883126e9h    */
129		seed[2] = ax;                   /* mov     seed+8,eax                   */
130		ax += 0x0702c49c - 0xc624dd2f;  /* add     eax,00702c49ch-0c624dd2fh    */
131		seed[3] = ax;                   /* mov     seed+12,eax                  */
132		ax += 0x9e353f7d - 0x0702c49c;  /* add     eax,09e353f7dh-00702c49ch    */
133		seed[4] = ax;                   /* mov     seed+16,eax                  */
134		ax += 0x6fdf3b64 - 0x9e353f7d;  /* add     eax,06fdf3b64h-09e353f7dh    */
135		seed[5] = ax;                   /* mov     seed+20,eax                  */
136	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/src/zh_copilot/replay/rng.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Zero Hour RNG and random general/color assignment.\n\nReproduces the engine's populateRandomSideAndColor() in pure Python so that\nreplays where players selected \"Random\" can have their actual general resolved\nwithout running the game engine.\n\nSource verified against:\n  GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/Common/RandomValue.cpp  (RNG)\n  GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/GameLogic/System/GameLogic.cpp (assignment)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# RNG — exact port of RandomValue.cpp\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _seed_random(seed_val: int) -> list[int]:\n    \"\"\"Initialize the 6-element uint32 RNG state from a single seed value.\n\n    Direct port of seedRandom() in RandomValue.cpp.\n    \"\"\"\n    M = 0xFFFFFFFF\n    ax = (seed_val + 0xf22d0e56) & M\n    s = [ax]\n    ax = (ax + 0x883126e9 - 0xf22d0e56) & M; s.append(ax)\n    ax = (ax + 0xc624dd2f - 0x883126e9) & M; s.append(ax)\n    ax = (ax + 0x0702c49c - 0xc624dd2f) & M; s.append(ax)\n    ax = (ax + 0x9e353f7d - 0x0702c49c) & M; s.append(ax)\n    ax = (ax + 0x6fdf3b64 - 0x9e353f7d) & M; s.append(ax)\n    return s\n\n\ndef _adc(a: int, b: int, c: int) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    \"\"\"32-bit add with carry. Returns (sum_uint32, carry).\n\n    Port of the ADC macro in RandomValue.cpp:\n        SUM = (A) + (B) + (C); C = ((SUM < (A)) || (SUM < (B)))\n    where all values are unsigned 32-bit.\n    \"\"\"\n    total = a + b + c\n    result = total & 0xFFFFFFFF\n    carry = 1 if (result < a or result < b) else 0\n    return result, carry\n\n\ndef _random_value(state: list[int]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Single RNG step. Mutates state in-place, returns a uint32.\n\n    Port of randomValue() in RandomValue.cpp.\n    \"\"\"\n    c = 0\n    ax, c = _adc(state[5], state[4], c); state[4] = ax\n    ax, c = _adc(ax, state[3], c);       state[3] = ax\n    ax, c = _adc(ax, state[2], c);       state[2] = ax\n    ax, c = _adc(ax, state[1], c);       state[1] = ax\n    ax, c = _adc(ax, state[0], c);       state[0] = ax\n\n    # Increment seed[5], bubbling carry up through seed[4..0]\n    state[5] = (state[5] + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF\n    if state[5] == 0:\n        state[4] = (state[4] + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF\n        if state[4] == 0:\n            state[3] = (state[3] + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF\n            if state[3] == 0:\n                state[2] = (state[2] + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF\n                if state[2] == 0:\n                    state[1] = (state[1] + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF\n                    if state[1] == 0:\n                        state[0] = (state[0] + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF\n                        ax = (ax + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF\n\n    return ax\n\n\ndef _rng_value(state: list[int], lo: int, hi: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Port of GetGameLogicRandomValue(lo, hi).\n\n    delta = hi - lo + 1; result = (randomValue() % delta) + lo\n    Uses unsigned arithmetic throughout.\n    \"\"\"\n    delta = hi - lo + 1\n    if delta == 0:\n        return hi\n    return int(_random_value(state) % delta) + lo\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Player template index → general/faction names\n# Matches PLAYER_TEMPLATE_MAP in game_options.py\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_TEMPLATE_TO_GENERAL: dict[int, tuple[str, str]] = {\n    2:  (\"America\",  \"America\"),\n    3:  (\"America\",  \"America Air Force\"),\n    4:  (\"America\",  \"America Laser\"),\n    5:  (\"America\",  \"America Superweapon\"),\n    6:  (\"China\",    \"China\"),\n    7:  (\"China\",    \"China Tank\"),\n    8:  (\"China\",    \"China Nuke\"),\n    9:  (\"China\",    \"China Infantry\"),\n    10: (\"GLA\",      \"GLA\"),\n    11: (\"GLA\",      \"GLA Demolitions\"),\n    12: (\"GLA\",      \"GLA Toxin\"),\n    13: (\"GLA\",      \"GLA Stealth\"),\n}\n\n# PlayerTemplate indices with starting buildings, in store order.\n# Standard ZH multiplayer (oldFactionsOnly=False): all 12 playable generals.\n# oldFactionsOnly=True: only the 3 base factions.\n_START_SLOTS_STANDARD    = list(range(2, 14))   # [2..13]\n_START_SLOTS_OLD_FACTIONS = [2, 6, 10]\n\n# ZH multiplayer has 8 colors (indices 0-7).\n_NUM_COLORS = 8\n\nPLAYERTEMPLATE_RANDOM   = -1\nPLAYERTEMPLATE_OBSERVER = -2\nMAX_SLOTS = 8\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SlotInfo:\n    \"\"\"Minimal per-slot data needed to reproduce general/color assignment.\"\"\"\n    occupied: bool\n    template: int   # -2=Observer, -1=Random, 0+=specific template\n    color: int      # -1=Random, 0-7=specific color\n\n\ndef resolve_random_generals(\n    seed: int,\n    slots: list[SlotInfo],\n    old_factions_only: bool = False,\n) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Simulate populateRandomSideAndColor() and return (faction, general) per slot.\n\n    For slots with a known template (>= 0), the header value is used directly.\n    For Random slots (-1), the result of the RNG simulation is returned.\n    Observer slots (-2) return (\"Observer\", \"Observer\").\n    Unoccupied slots return (\"\", \"\").\n\n    Matches the exact algorithm in GameLogic.cpp:populateRandomSideAndColor()\n    with #define MORE_RANDOM active (which it always is in the shipped game).\n    \"\"\"\n    state = _seed_random(seed)\n    start_slots = _START_SLOTS_OLD_FACTIONS if old_factions_only else _START_SLOTS_STANDARD\n    n_generals = len(start_slots)\n\n    # silly = fixed number of warm-up RNG discards per Random player (seed % 7)\n    silly = seed % 7\n\n    # Track which colors are already taken (pre-assigned + newly assigned)\n    taken_colors: set[int] = {s.color for s in slots if s.color >= 0}\n\n    results: list[tuple[str, str]] = []\n\n    for slot in slots:\n        if not slot.occupied:\n            results.append((\"\", \"\"))\n            continue\n\n        if slot.template == PLAYERTEMPLATE_OBSERVER:\n            results.append((\"Observer\", \"Observer\"))\n            # Observers still get color assigned if needed\n            if slot.color < 0:\n                color = -1\n                while color == -1:\n                    c = _rng_value(state, 0, _NUM_COLORS - 1)\n                    if c not in taken_colors:\n                        color = c\n                taken_colors.add(color)\n            continue\n\n        # --- General assignment ---\n        template = slot.template\n        if template < 0:  # Random\n            # Warm-up discards\n            for _ in range(silly):\n                _rng_value(state, 0, 1)\n            idx = _rng_value(state, 0, 1000) % n_generals\n            template = start_slots[idx]\n\n        faction, general = _TEMPLATE_TO_GENERAL.get(template, (\"Unknown\", f\"template_{template}\"))\n        results.append((faction, general))\n\n        # --- Color assignment ---\n        if slot.color < 0:\n            color = -1\n            while color == -1:\n                c = _rng_value(state, 0, _NUM_COLORS - 1)\n                if c not in taken_colors:\n                    color = c\n            taken_colors.add(color)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef parse_game_seed(options_str: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Extract the SD= seed value from the game options string.\"\"\"\n    for token in options_str.split(\";\"):\n        if token.startswith(\"SD=\"):\n            try:\n                return int(token[3:])\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n    return None\n\n\ndef parse_old_factions_flag(options_str: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Extract the O= (oldFactionsOnly) flag from the game options string.\"\"\"\n    for token in options_str.split(\";\"):\n        if token.startswith(\"O=\"):\n            return token[3:].strip().upper() == \"Y\"\n    return False\n"
}
